However, standard flow matching treats all spatial frequencies uniformly, ignoring the natural frequency hierarchy where high-frequency bands become indistinguishable from pure noise far earlier than coarse structures. We introduce WaiT, a Wavelet-aware image Transformer that decomposes generation into coarse and fine bands via lossless wa","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-30T18:26:19Z","category_id":"multimodal_gen","cover_url":"/covers/2607_28760.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV","cs.AI","cs.LG","stat.ML"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的问题很具体：标准 flow matching 在整条生成轨迹上对所有空间频率一视同仁，但图像里的低频结构和高频纹理并不等价。高频分量在较强噪声下会更早退化成近似纯噪声，模型如果从一开始就同时建模高低频，早期大量算力会花在几乎不可辨认的高频上，结果是全局结构建立阶段被干扰，采样也不够经济。作者把这个问题表述为“高频应该等到有信号时再进入生成过程”，核心是在不引入复杂多尺度架构的前提下，把频率层级直接写进 flow matching 的噪声日程里。","importance":"对预训练研究的启发主要有两层。第一，它说明生成目标本身可以带有结构化归纳偏置，不一定非要靠更复杂的 backbone 或 latent VAE 才能获得多尺度建模能力。这里用的是无损小波分解，把 coarse-to-fine 的先验直接放进训练和采样过程。第二，它对统一建模范式有参考价值：如果不同子空间的信噪比演化规律不同，就不该共享完全相同的 corruption / denoising schedule。这一点不只适用于图像，也可能迁移到视频、音频离散 token、甚至某些非 AR 语言建模范式。就量级看，这不是“换个指标的小修小补”，而是在 pixel-space flow matching 上给出了一种简单但有效的训练-采样改造，同时把评测也往更细粒度推进了一步。","technical_approach":"方法的关键是把图像先做单层离散小波变换 DWT，拆成一个低频近似带和三个高频细节带。作者全程使用 Haar wavelet，因为它实现简单、正交、可逆，而且单层 2D Haar 在直觉上接近 2×2 average pooling 加残差信息存储。与 learned latent 不同，这个变换是无损的，不需要额外训练编码器。\n\n在此基础上，作者没有设计双塔或金字塔网络，而是沿用 JiT 的 pixel-space transformer，只改噪声注入和采样日程。低频带在整个 t∈[0,1] 上参与 flow matching；高频带只在 t∈[t*,1] 上参与，也就是前半段保持纯噪声，等低频已经形成较稳定的粗结构后，再把高频噪声注入并与低频通过逆小波变换合并，随后联合去噪。这样做的含义很明确：早期模型只处理 coarse structure，后期再处理 fine detail，避免在低信噪比区间浪费容量。\n\n从训练角度看，这相当于给不同频带定义不同的线性噪声日程。作者强调这不是把高频完全独立建模，而是“延迟进入、后期联合 refinement”，因此仍保留跨频带耦合。相比 latent diffusion，这个方案不需要 VAE 压缩带来的信息损失；相比标准 pixel-space flow matching，它显式利用了自然图像的频率层级；相比复杂 progressive 生成架构，它几乎不改主干网络，工程代价较低。\n\n另一个值得注意的点是评测设计。作者认为标准 FID 会把高分辨率图像下采样到 299×299，天然忽略很多高频纹理，因此补充了 5-crop FID 用于原生分辨率局部结构评估，以及 high-frequency Fréchet Wavelet Distance，用来更直接地看纹理保真度。这个评测设计和方法本身是配套的，因为如果目标就是改善高频建模，只看 FID 很容易低估收益。","experiments":"实验覆盖 ImageNet、OpenImages、text-to-image 和 video，重点结果比较完整。\n\n在 ImageNet 512×512 上，作者报告 WaiT 达到 FID 1.43、5cFID 1.63、hFWD 0.67，并且相对 JiT baseline 最高可减少 50% 采样 GFLOPs。最大的 2B 模型在 ImageNet 512 上做到 pixel-space 模型里 1.3 的 FID。文中还强调，在 hFWD 这个纯纹理指标上，WaiT 超过了当时的 pixel-space 和 latent-space 方法，说明收益不只是全局结构更稳，而是高频细节确实更好。\n\n在 OpenImages 的 512 和 1024 分辨率条件生成上，趋势一致，说明方法不是只对 ImageNet 这种相对规整的数据有效。迁移到 pixel-space text-to-image 1024×1024 时，作者称在几乎所有指标上持平或优于 JiT，同时吞吐最高可到 3 倍。再往视频扩展时，使用时空小波，Kinetics-600 上 FVD 达到 0.84。\n\n从消融逻辑看，最值得注意的不是单个绝对数，而是几组机制验证：一是作者通过轨迹分析展示高频在早期时间步几乎不可辨认，支持“wait for the signal”的前提；二是不同 t* 设定体现了质量与速度的折中，说明延迟过早或过晚都不是最优；三是三轴指标之间并不完全一致，某些方法 FID 好但 hFWD 差，证明只看 FID 会漏掉纹理质量问题；四是人类偏好与 5cFID/hFWD 的相关性被用来支撑新评测的有效性。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，但如果你关心非 AR 生成建模范式，建议至少把方法和评测部分看细一点。优先看 Introduction 和方法总览图，先建立“为什么高频要延迟”的直觉。然后看 Figure 3 一类的轨迹可视化，那里给出了高频在早期近似纯噪声的证据，这是整篇成立的前提。方法部分重点看 DWT 分解、双日程定义、以及推理时在 t* 注入高频噪声再联合去噪的流程。实验部分建议重点看 ImageNet 512 的主表、t* 或频带设计的消融、以及三轴评测和人类偏好的对应关系。预计 25 到 35 分钟可以抓住主要信息。\n\n如果从预训练研究角度深挖，有两个点值得继续想。第一，频率分组只是一个特例，更一般的问题是：不同子空间是否应该共享同一 corruption schedule。第二，lossless transform + delayed denoising 这种思路，能否迁移到离散 token 或多模态统一生成里，用来处理不同模态、不同码本层级、不同语义粒度的信噪比不匹配。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a specific inefficiency in standard flow matching: it applies the same denoising schedule to all spatial frequencies, even though low-frequency structure and high-frequency detail behave very differently under noise. High-frequency components become indistinguishable from noise much earlier than coarse structure. As a result, a substantial part of the early trajectory is spent modeling HF bands that carry little usable signal, exactly when the model should be allocating capacity to global layout and coherence. The proposed fix is to let high frequencies “wait for the signal” rather than participate from the start.","importance":"For pretraining-oriented readers, the main value is not image generation performance alone. The paper shows that a generative objective can encode a useful structural prior directly in the corruption and denoising process, without relying on a more elaborate backbone or a learned latent autoencoder. That is a broader lesson: if different subspaces have different signal-to-noise dynamics, forcing them to share one schedule is likely suboptimal. This idea could matter beyond image FM, including video, audio token modeling, and alternative LM paradigms that move away from plain next-token AR. We revisit this problem through the geometry of rectified flows: along the straight interpolation between noise and data, the Bayes-optimal velocity is governed to leading order at the noise end by a few class-conditional moments, and increasingly sample-specific structure matters toward the data end. SciThèque compiles hypotheses, datasets, hi","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-31T03:34:39Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2607_28990.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这项工作要解决的是科学数据分析 Agent 的训练接口问题：真实科学数据很多，但通常只有静态数据文件，缺少可执行环境、可验证目标和跨 turn 的过程监督。结果是 Agent 只能靠提示词或通用多轮数据学习分析流程，很难学到什么时候查数据、什么时候选统计方法、什么时候修正假设、什么时候提交结论。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接价值不在基座训练配方，而在 post-train 训练信号设计。SciDisco 把科学发现任务改造成可执行、可验证、可给过程奖励的环境，这对 agentic RL 很关键。它强调的不是最终答案奖励，而是 turn-level credit assignment：每一步是否产生了可验证的分析证据。这个思路对代码 Agent、数据分析 Agent、长程工具使用 RL 都有参考价值，尤其适合思考如何从环境中构造更密集、更可信的训练信号。","technical_approach":"方法分三层。第一层是 SciThèque，把开放科学数据集编译成带有 hypothesis、dataset、sandbox execution 和 verifier 的任务环境。环境对 Agent 暴露任务目标和数据，并执行代码动作；任务内部保留 hidden evidence DAG，用来描述从数据理解到统计证据再到最终结论的可验证路径。第二层是 DAG-grounded trajectory synthesis，用 hidden evidence DAG 合成多轮演示轨迹，并通过 verifier 过滤，作为 SFT 冷启动数据。这样做是为了避免 RL 初期大量无效工具调用和错误变量读取。第三层是 DiscoPO，用环境反馈给每个 turn 分配过程奖励，而不是只在最后提交答案时给稀疏奖励。核心区别在于，任务构造、SFT 数据生成和 RL 过程奖励都共享同一个 evidence DAG 接口，减少了演示数据和在线优化目标之间的不一致。","experiments":"给定内容主要覆盖问题定义和方法设计，没有提供完整实验表、性能数字或消融结果。可确认的实验相关设定包括：任务被建模为 POMDP，Agent 与每个科学发现环境多轮交互；动作包括可执行分析和终止提交；奖励从最终稀疏奖励扩展为 turn-level process reward。真正需要看的实验应包括 DiscoPO 相比纯 prompt、SFT、final-reward RL 的收益，DAG 合成轨迹的冷启动效果，以及 process verifier 对长程分析成功率的影响。但具体参数量、任务规模和提升幅度在提供内容中不可见。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，预计 20–30 分钟。重点看 Problem Formulation 中把科学发现写成 POMDP 和过程奖励目标的部分，以及 Methodology 中 hidden evidence DAG 如何同时服务于任务构建、轨迹合成和 RL 奖励。若只能看一处，优先看 SciThèque 与 DiscoPO 的接口设计。对基座研究员来说，最值得深挖的是 verifier 能否稳定表达“有效分析进展”，以及这种 verifier 是否会诱导 Agent 学会迎合检查器而不是真正改进分析能力。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The work addresses a training-interface problem for scientific data-analysis agents. Open scientific datasets are abundant, but they usually do not come with executable task environments, verifiable goals, or process-level supervision across turns. As a result, agents trained with prompts or generic multi-turn traces often fail to learn robust policies for inspecting data, choosing statistical methods, revising analyses, and submitting evidence-backed claims.","importance":"Its main relevance to foundation-model research is not a pretraining recipe, but the design of post-training signals for agentic RL. SciDisco turns scientific discovery into an executable and verifiable environment where reward can be assigned at the turn level. This is useful for thinking about long-horizon tool-use training: the training signal should identify which intermediate actions produced valid evidence, rather than rewarding only the final answer. The same design pattern is applicable to coding agents, data-analysis agents, and other environments where sparse outcome rewards are too weak.","technical_approach":"The pipeline has three components. SciThèque compiles open scientific datasets into hypothesis-driven environments with sandboxed code execution and task-specific verifiers. Each task contains a hidden evidence DAG that represents verifiable analytical progress toward the target finding. DAG-grounded trajectory synthesis uses this structure to generate multi-turn demonstrations and filters them through the verifier, providing a behavioral prior before RL. DiscoPO then optimizes the policy using process feedback from the environment, assigning credit to turns that advance the hidden evidence state. The key design choice is that task construction, SFT trajectory generation, and online RL credit assignment all share the same evidence-DAG interface.","experiments":"The supplied content covers the formulation and method, but does not include complete experimental tables, numerical results, or ablations. The confirmed setup models each task as a POMDP: the agent receives the hypothesis and dataset, interacts through executable actions, observes environment feedback, and eventually submits a claim. Reward is extended from a sparse final signal to turn-level process rewards. The missing but important experimental evidence would be comparisons against prompting, SFT-only training, and final-reward RL, plus ablations on DAG-grounded demonstrations and process verification.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20–30 minutes. Focus on the POMDP and process-reward formulation, then read the SciThèque and DiscoPO interface design. The most useful part for pretraining and post-training researchers is the hidden evidence DAG: how it defines valid progress, how it supports synthetic demonstrations, and how it becomes a reward source during online RL. The main question to keep in mind is whether the verifier captures real analytical progress or merely creates a new target for reward hacking.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"可过程验证环境比最终答案 RL 更接近可扩展监督；只看 SciThèque/DiscoPO 设计和 verifier 噪声处理。","en":"Process-verifiable environments are closer to scalable supervision than final-answer RL; focus on SciThèque, DiscoPO, and how verifier noise is handled."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Yucheng Xu","Keyi Zhang","Yuyang Yu","Min Zhang","Shiyuan Meng","Pei Chu","Zhongying Tu"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["agentic-rl","process-supervision","rlvr","scientific-discovery","verifiers"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28990","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2607.28990","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2607.29246","title":"Don't Mix Rewards, Mix Policies: Policy Decomposition and Optimization for Multi-Reward RL","abstract":"Modern large language models (LLMs) are expected not just to answer correctly, but to adapt their behavior to different human values and use cases. As a result, multi-reward reinforcement learning (RL) has become an increasingly important problem for LLMs, where each reward captures a different aspect of desired behavior. However, optimizing with multiple rewards suffers from a more severe alignment tax issue, where different optimization objectives can trade off or even conflict with each other","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-31T10:19:41Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2607_29246.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是多奖励 RL 中一个很具体、也很常见的问题：当 helpfulness、safety、correctness、tool-use success 这类奖励被先加权成一个标量再做策略优化时，不同目标会在同一步梯度里直接冲突，结果是每个目标都学得不够彻底，且对权重、尺度、奖励校准都很敏感。作者把这个现象明确命名为 multi-reward alignment tax。论文要解决的不是“如何再设计一个更好的 reward weighting”，而是换掉 reward-space composition 这个接口本身：不要先混奖励，再训一个折中策略；而是分别学每个奖励对应的正向策略，再在策略空间里组合。","importance":"对基座研究的直接意义不在 pretrain 主体，而在 pretrain↔post-train 接口。现在很多后训练问题都不是单一目标，尤其是 reasoning、tool use、safety、style control 同时存在时，reward scalarization 往往带来不稳定和性能折损。PRISM 提供了一个很清楚的替代思路：把“偏好组合”从 reward 侧移到 policy 侧，把训练时的冲突拆开，把推理时的权衡显式化。这个思路对多目标对齐、可控推理、甚至未来把不同能力头或不同 mode 合并进统一模型都有启发。贡献量级上，它更像一个有明确机制假设、并在多个设置下验证有效的后训练方法，而不是会立刻改写主流 RL recipe 的基础定律型工作。","technical_approach":"方法核心是 policy decomposition + logit-space composition。具体做法有三层。\n\n第一层，策略分解。对 N 个奖励，作者不再训练一个共享的单策略去同时最大化所有奖励，而是训练 N 个 positive policies，每个只负责一个 reward 维度；另外再训练一个 global negative policy，用来统一建模“所有不希望出现的行为”。这里的设计判断很关键：正向行为通常是 reward-specific 的，例如数学正确、工具调用格式正确、安全拒答，它们需要各自独立的优化方向；负向行为则可以合并，因为很多 failure mode 在“不要做”这个层面是可共享的。\n\n第二层，参数共享实现。为了避免真训练 N+1 个完整模型，PRISM 用一个共享 LM，加不同 prefix token 来条件化出不同子策略。也就是说，底座参数共享，子策略通过前缀路由到不同 mode。这样训练和部署成本都可控，也让不同子策略之间保留一定共享表示。\n\n第三层，组合方式。采样和推理时，不是选一个子策略，而是把各子策略的 logits 做加权组合，形成 mixture policy。论文强调 merge weights 是显式、可解释、可在 inference-time 调整的偏好接口。和 reward weighting 不同，这些权重不再直接作用于 noisy reward scale，而是作用于已经各自优化过的策略分布，因此更稳定，也更容易做 trade-off control。\n\n训练上，作者以 GRPO 为基础更新器。每个 reward 主要驱动其对应 positive branch 的更新，negative branch 则吸收各 reward 下的失败样本信号。这样做的目标是减少 conflicting rewards 在同一梯度中的直接干扰。和线性标量化、动态加权、Lagrangian constraint 类方法相比，区别不在 advantage estimator，而在“优化接口”从 reward scalar 转成了 policy mixture。全文里值得注意的一点是，作者并不是否认 Pareto trade-off 存在，而是认为把 trade-off 放到训练后、推理时的策略组合层面，比在训练时把所有目标压成一个标量更合适。","experiments":"实验覆盖三类多奖励场景：科学问答、工具调用、helpfulness-safety 对齐。\n\n1. 科学问答：在 GPQA 和 ScienceQA 上，PRISM 在所有 backbone 上都拿到最优 overall score。相对最强 baseline，分别在 DeepSeek-R1-1.5B、Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct、Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct 上提升 17.8、8.0、0.6 分。这个结果说明方法收益在小模型和较强指令模型上都存在，但增益幅度会随底座和任务难度变化。\n\n2. 工具调用：在 BFCL-v3 上，PRISM 排名第一。这里多奖励通常对应任务成功、格式合法、工具选择正确、可能还包括安全约束。该场景对方法是较强检验，因为 reward 冲突更真实，不只是“答对/答错”。\n\n3. 安全对齐：在 Alpaca、HH-RLHF、PKU-SafeRLHF 这组 helpfulness–safety benchmark 上，PRISM 同样优于其他 RL 方法，并且随着 reward complexity 增加，鲁棒性和 controllability 更好。论文的叙述重点不是单点分数，而是当奖励数量增加、目标冲突变强时，reward-space baselines 更容易退化。\n\n从消融角度，最值得关注的是几类比较：\n- policy-space composition vs reward scalarization：验证核心命题；\n- 是否需要 global negative policy：检验“共享负向分支”是否真能吸收 failure union；\n- 不同 merge weights 的 inference-time controllability：看是否能平滑调节偏好而不重训；\n- reward complexity 增加时的稳定性：这是论文机制主张最关键的证据。\n\n摘要里没有给出全部训练 token、rollout group size、各 reward 具体定义和每项消融数字，但从正文结构看，作者是围绕“性能更高 + 冲突更少 + 控制更稳”三条线组织证据的，而不是只报一个 aggregate score。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先抓方法假设，再看实验是否真的支撑这个假设。\n\n优先看 Introduction 和方法总图，先弄清作者到底在替换什么接口：不是换 RL 算法外壳，而是把 reward composition 改成 policy composition。这个判断如果没抓住，后面会误以为只是“多头 GRPO”。\n\n接着看方法部分里三个点：\n1. positive policies + global negative policy 的形式化定义；\n2. prefix-conditioned parameter sharing 怎么实现；\n3. logit-level composition 在训练 rollout 和推理时如何一致使用。\n\n实验部分建议重点看：\n- GPQA/ScienceQA 上不同 backbone 的提升是否一致；\n- BFCL-v3 和 safety benchmarks 上，方法是否在 reward 冲突更强时更占优；\n- controllability 图，尤其是 merge weight 调节曲线是否平滑、是否出现某一维奖励突然塌陷。\n\n如果时间有限，30–40 分钟足够把核心思想和主要证据看明白。若要深挖，建议重点想两个问题：第一，prefix-conditioned sub-policy 是否真的足以解耦梯度冲突，还是只是软隔离；第二，global negative policy 会不会把本来 reward-specific 的负例过度合并，导致边界模糊。这两个点决定方法能否扩展到更多奖励、更大模型和更复杂 agent setting。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a specific failure mode in multi-reward RL for LLM alignment: when rewards such as helpfulness, safety, correctness, and tool-use success are scalarized into a single objective, the resulting gradient step forces incompatible preferences to compete inside one update. The authors call this the multi-reward alignment tax. Their goal is not to design a better reward weighting rule, but to replace reward-space composition itself. Instead of mixing rewards first and training one compromise policy, they learn separate reward-specific positive policies and compose them in policy space.","importance":"Its relevance is mainly at the pretrain–post-train interface rather than pretraining proper. Modern post-training increasingly involves multiple objectives, and scalarized reward optimization often becomes brittle as objectives conflict or differ in scale. PRISM offers a clean alternative: separate optimization by preference dimension during training, then expose trade-offs explicitly at inference through policy mixing. That is useful for multi-objective alignment, controllable behavior, and potentially for future systems that combine different capability modes inside one shared model. This is better read as a strong methodological proposal with a clear mechanism hypothesis than as a new general law of RL training.","technical_approach":"$1b","experiments":"$1c","reading_guide":"$1d","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"值得看 §方法和 ablation：policy-space 分解若稳定，内部多 reward RL 不该只调权重。","en":"Read the method and ablations: if policy-space decomposition is stable, internal multi-reward RL should not rely only on weight tuning."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Ruiming Liang","Yi Zhong","Yizhen Yuan","Yinan Zheng","Tianyi Tan","Tianyue Wang","Haiyun Guo","Jinqiao Wang","Xianyuan Zhan"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["multi-reward-RL","RLHF","policy-composition","alignment-tax"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29246","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2607.29246","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2607.29209","title":"SAF-OPD: Stable Advantage Fusion for On-Policy Distillation","abstract":"Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) broadcasts a single response-level reward to every token, while on-policy distillation (OPD) scores each token against a stronger teacher for a dense advantage but caps performance at teacher quality and discourages exploration beyond it. Their complementarity makes combining RLVR and OPD promising, but we find that fusing the two advantages with a fixed coefficient triggers entropy collapse from two miscalibrations: a magnitude mismatch, whe","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-31T09:28:16Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2607_29209.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"RLVR（以GRPO为代表）的响应级稀疏优势与on-policy蒸馏（OPD）的token级稠密优势直接用固定系数融合时，会触发训练早期熵崩溃，根源是两个独立失配：一是量级失配，OPD的token级优势无界且重尾，会数值上盖过有界的RLVR优势信号；二是时间失配，训练全程保持全强度OPD会持续将学生拉向教师分布，限制学生探索并超越教师的能力，单一固定系数无法同时解决这两个不同维度的问题。","importance":"属于后训练对齐阶段RLVR与蒸馏融合方向的实用方法改进，为RLHF/RLVR流水线中的优势融合提供了可直接落地的稳定策略，避免了单一融合系数调参的盲区，对做对齐训练的工程师有直接参考价值。","technical_approach":"提出SAF（Stable Advantage Fusion）框架，用两个独立机制分别对应两种失配：1）token级量级控制器：对单条响应内的OPD优势分布做稀疏化和幅值截断，消除重尾token对更新的主导作用；2）训练阶段控制器：在线跟踪学生与教师的KL散度，检测OPD信号作用耗尽的节点，逐步退火OPD的全局系数，仅在指导有效时保持全强度。方法无需额外模型或辅助损失，仅对采样得到的OPD优势做轻量变换即可接入现有训练流程。","experiments":"诊断实验验证了固定系数融合基线的失效模式：训练早期策略熵即崩溃并维持低位，学生被过度对齐到教师分布，最终精度低于可控融合方案；进一步验证了量级失配与时间失配分别作用于token级尺度和训练时间尺度两个独立维度，仅靠调整单一全局系数无法同时解决两个问题。","reading_guide":"泛读重点看第3章SAF的四阶段融合规则细节，以及第4.4节的失配诊断实验与消融分析，预计阅读时间15-20分钟。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Direct fixed-coefficient fusion of sparse response-level advantages from RLVR (e.g. GRPO) and dense token-level advantages from on-policy distillation (OPD) triggers early training entropy collapse. The failure stems from two independent mismatches: magnitude mismatch, where unbounded heavy-tailed token-level OPD advantages numerically overwhelm bounded RLVR signals; and temporal mismatch, where full-strength OPD applied throughout training continuously pulls the student toward the teacher distribution, limiting exploration beyond teacher quality. A single fixed coefficient cannot address both mismatches across their distinct axes.","importance":"Practical method improvement for RLVR-distillation fusion in post-training alignment, providing a deployable stabilization strategy for advantage mixing in RLHF/RLVR pipelines and eliminating blind spots in single-coefficient tuning. Directly relevant for engineers working on alignment training workflows.","technical_approach":"Proposes SAF (Stable Advantage Fusion), a framework with two dedicated mechanisms matched to each mismatch: 1) Token-level magnitude controller: sparsifies and bounds the per-response OPD advantage distribution to eliminate heavy-tailed token dominance in updates; 2) Training-stage controller: tracks online student-teacher KL divergence to detect when OPD guidance is no longer useful, then anneals the global OPD coefficient to maintain full strength only while guidance remains effective. The method requires no additional models or auxiliary losses, and can be integrated into existing pipelines as a lightweight transformation of sampled OPD advantages.","experiments":"Diagnostic experiments confirm the failure mode of fixed-coefficient fusion baselines: policy entropy collapses early in training and stays low, the student is over-aligned to the teacher, and final accuracy falls short of controlled fusion schemes. Further experiments verify that magnitude mismatch (token-level scale) and temporal mismatch (training-time schedule) operate on independent axes, so retuning a single global coefficient cannot resolve both issues.","reading_guide":"Skim Section 3 for details of the four-stage SAF fusion rule, and Section 4.4 for mismatch diagnosis experiments and ablation studies. Li","Ziheng Li","Yuquan Lu","Siyu Zhang","Dongsheng Ma","Rongxiang Weng","Xunliang Cai","Yun Chen"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["RLVR","on-policy distillation","entropy collapse","post-training","advantage fusion"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29209","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2607.29209","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2607.28669","title":"LARA: Lightweight Adapters in the Residual Stream for Composable Adaptation and Alignment","abstract":"We present LARA (Lightweight Additive Residual Adaptation), a method for efficient adaptation that operates in the residual stream of a frozen model rather than in its weights. Where LoRA adds an update of low rank to weight matrices, LARA reads the hidden state at a small set of layers and adds a correction of low rank back to the residual stream, leaving all base weights untouched. On a code fine-tuning task and on preference optimization (DPO), LARA matches LoRA at equal parameter counts. Key properties: comparable performance to LoRA at equal parameter budgets; inference-time control of adaptation strength via a single scalar coefficient γ, enabling smooth interpolation between base and adapted behavior; support for multiple independent adaptation modules on one frozen base, with per-token routing to select behaviors at a small fraction of the memory cost of full model copies.","experiments":"Parameter-matched comparisons on code fine-tuning (SFT) and preference optimization (DPO) tasks: LARA is configured with 6 inserted layers, rank 128, hidden size 1536, with ~2.38M trainable parameters; LoRA is configured with rank 16 updates on Q/V projections across all layers, with ~2.18M trainable parameters. audit。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper tests a common but rarely isolated assumption: adding more interventional data to pretraining should help a language model learn causal direction. The authors place this question in a fully controlled synthetic SCM setting and deliberately construct Simpson’s-paradox worlds where observational correlation and true causal effect have opposite signs. This makes it possible to separate “learning correlation” from “learning causal direction” with minimal ambiguity. The main result is negative in a precise way: increasing the intervention fraction in training does not reliably improve directional accuracy. What improves is the magnitude of the model’s do-response, while the sign remains copied from observational context. The core finding is therefore a magnitude–direction split: intervention helps the model estimate response strength, but contextual observational evidence still dominates directional judgment at inference time.","importance":"$1f","technical_approach":"$20","experiments":"$21","reading_guide":"Start with the introduction, especially the definition of H1, the magnitude–direction duality, and the claim that evidence type at inference dominates mixture ratio in training. disclos","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-31T06:30:45Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2607_29062.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这项工作研究 之间的迁移性较强。这说明相关表征可能不是狭窄的 cue 模板特征，而更接近“承认外部线索影响”的通用方向。","technical_approach":"实验使用 residual stream activation steering。作者比较四类向量构造方式：第一是 labeled on-policy contrastive vector，用模型在带 cue prompt 上生成的轨迹，经 judge 标注为承认 cue 或不承认 cue 后，对两类平均激活做差；第二是 synthetic vector，用模板化的 cue-acknowledging 和 cue-ignoring completion 代替真实 rollout，因此不需要模型采样和 judge 标签；第三是 cue-specific optimized vector，通过梯度优化一个方向，使被 steering 的模型提高特定 cue 承认文本的似然；第四是 cue-agnostic optimized direction，优化目标不点名具体 cue，测试是否存在更通用的 faithfulness 方向。评估时区分 efficacy generalization 和 transfer generalization：前者看某个 setting 下 steering 是否有效，后者看在一个 setting hint、unethical insider tip，均指向错误选项。GPQA 上改变 cue 类型；固定 Stanford cue 时改变数据集。每个 scenario 保留非退化样本，不要求模型一定受 cue 影响，也不要求无 cue 时答案不同；训练和测试约 67/33 划分，每个 scenario 测试集约 129–165 个样本。生成使用 greedy decoding，并在同一模型内匹配 token limit 和 penalty。主要结果是：steering 稳定提升 cue acknowledgment 的范围较窄，主要出现在 Gemma-3 12B，且受数据集影响明显；但在 GPQA 上，一个 cue 训练出的向量与目标 cue 自身训练出的向量效果接近；固定 cue 25–35 分钟。优先看 Setup and Methods 中四种 vector 构造方式，以及 efficacy generalization 和 transfer generalization 的定义。结果部分建议重点看按模型大小拆分的表格或图，因为小模型和 12B 模型的差异是结论的边界。还应看 cue use / hidden cue use 分析，这是判断 steering 是否真的提高 faithfulness，而不是简单诱导模型更多使用 cue 的关键。对基座研究员来说，值得关注的是 synthetic vector 不依赖 rollout 和 judge 标签却能接近 contrastive vector，这暗示某些推理披露方向可能可以用很轻的数据构造出来。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The work asks whether activation steering for chain-of-thought faithfulness generalizes. If a steering vector is built for one cue type, dataset, or construction procedure, does it still make the model disclose influential prompt cues in other settings?","importance":"The main value is in mechanistic analysis and monitorable reasoning. CoT monitoring is only useful when the model verbalizes the factors that drive its answer. If a model follows a misleading cue but hides that influence in its reasoning, the monitor loses signal. This study moves faithfulness steering beyond a single experimental setting and tests variation across models, datasets, cue types, and vector construction methods. The result is nuanced: efficacy is narrow and reliable mainly for Gemma-3 12B, while transfer is broad once the effect exists. This suggests that the steered feature is not merely a memorized cue template, but may correspond to a more general tendency to acknowledge external cue influence.","technical_approach":"The method applies residual-stream activation steering and compares four ways to construct the steering direction. The contrastive vector is the difference between mean activations from judge-labeled cue-acknowledging and cue-ignoring on-policy rollouts. The synthetic vector replaces rollouts with templated cue-acknowledging and cue-ignoring completions, avoiding both model sampling and judge labels. The cue-specific optimized vector is learned by gradient descent so that the steered model assigns higher likelihood to cue-acknowledging target completions. The cue-agnostic optimized direction uses a target that does not name a particular cue, testing whether a more generic faithfulness direction exists. The evaluation separates efficacy generalization, meaning whether steering works at all in a given setting, from transfer generalization, meaning whether a vector built in one setting remains useful in another.","experiments":"$22","reading_guide":"Skim in about 25–35 minutes. Read the setup carefully, especially the four vector construction methods and the distinction between efficacy and transfer generalization. In the results, focus on model-wise breakdowns, since the contrast between smaller models and Gemma-3 12B defines the boundary of the claim. Also read the cue-use versus hidden-cue-use analysis; it is the key check that steering improves disclosure rather than simply making the model rely more on the cue. For foundation-model researchers, the most interesting detail is that synthetic vectors can approach labeled contrastive vectors without rollouts or judge labels.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"结论会削弱“一个 steering vector 到处管用”的乐观 prior；看跨 cue 泛化表和最大模型对比。","en":"This weakens the optimistic prior that one steering vector transfers broadly; read the cross-cue generalization table and the largest-model comparison."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Matthew Nguyen","Kyle Cox","Austin Meek","Iván Arcuschin"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["cot-faithfulness","activation-steering","mechanistic-analysis","reasoning","model-safety"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29062","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2607.29062","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2607.29617","title":"When Does On-Policy Interaction Help? Representational Tradeoffs in Value-Based Imitation Learning","abstract":"Imitation learning (IL)---training an agent to replicate expert behavior from demonstrations---underpins applications from robotics to language model training. Standard approaches such as Behavior Cloning (BC) are known to suffer from compounding errors and performance plateaus, particularly when the learner cannot perfectly represent the expert's policy (as is typical, e.g., in distillation). The framework supports three settings: self-revision, peer-revision (same agent type), and cross-agent revision (humans revise LLM outputs and vice versa). An information-theoretic framework uses per-iteration cross-entropy reduction (information gain ΔI) to quantitatively characterize the revision process, distinguishing information accumulation from random reshuffling of the output distribution. Experiments cover two finite-answer-space tasks (4-class math reasoning MalAlgoQA, 10-class sentiment IMDb-Rating) and one open-ended temporal reasoning task (TISER), comparing behavior across 5 mainstream LLMs and non-expert human annotators.","experiments":"LLM reflection exhibits two failure modes, unified by an information-theoretic principle: without external information, self-conditioning cannot reduce the conditional entropy of the target variable. Specifically: on objective reasoning tasks with finite answer spaces, self-revision yields near-zero information gain (ΔI≈0), equivalent to neutral re-generation with no accumulation of task-relevant information; on subjective tasks, information gain is negative (ΔI<0). Human revision under identical conditions yields positive information gain. Cross-agent revision matrices localize the failure to the revision step itself, not the quality of the input being revised. The dominant bottleneck (error detection vs. error correction) varies across tasks and models, with no single universal failure mechanism.","reading_guide":"Skim read. expli","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-19T17:09:32Z","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2607_28638.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文解决 LLM agent 从交互经验中学习时过度依赖 trajectory-level reflection 的问题。传统反思把一整条轨迹事后总结成经验，容易把环境规律和偶然路径混在一起；作者主张把知识表示为 state-grounded predictive knowledge，即每个状态下对未来结果、转移风险、子目标距离或机会窗口的显式预测。","importance":"这篇更接近 agent learning / RL-style post-training，对基座预训练的启发在于经验数据应如何组织成可学习信号。它强调训练样本不应只是“成功/失败轨迹 + 反思文本”，而应把状态、动作、后继结果和预测性声明绑定起来。这对构造 agent SFT/RL 数据、长期记忆、环境交互式持续学习有参考意义。它也提示一个更一般的问题：LLM 从经验中学到的不是越长的总结越好，而是要能在相同或相似状态上复用的预测。","technical_approach":"方法叫 Stateful Knowledge Learning（SKL）。agent 维护一个按状态索引的知识表 {z_s}，每个 z_s 是对当前状态未来结果的显式声明。与 Reflexion 类方法不同，SKL 不在完整 trajectory 结束后生成全局 critique，而是在多次 trial 中收集同一状态附近的后续片段 {τ_n^H(s)}，再聚合这些 state-specific evidence 更新 z_s。决策时，policy 以当前状态 s 和对应知识 z_s 为条件选择动作。作者还提出两个可扩展训练版本：SKL-SD 用 self-distillation 训练模型从经验中抽取和更新 stateful knowledge；SKL-RL 用强化学习进一步训练 agent 通过状态级经验聚合和 predictive bootstrapping 改进策略。predictive bootstrapping 的含义是，后继状态已经学到的预测可以反向传播到前序状态，形成更细粒度的经验复用。","experiments":"论文用一个 stochastic FrozenLake 例子解释机制：在带滑动随机性的环境里，“早期不要向右走”这类轨迹级经验不稳定，而“当坑在相邻位置时，向右有风险”这类状态绑定预测更容易泛化。规模化实验覆盖 WebShop、ScienceWorld 和 contamination-free ChessPuzzles。正文片段给出的结论是，SKL 系列方法相较 reflection-based training paradigms 有更好的表现，尤其体现为更细粒度的泛化和知识自举能力；所给内容未包含完整表格中的具体提升数字，因此不应额外推断幅度。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，约 25–35 分钟。优先看 Section 2 的 motivating example 和 Figure 1，因为那里最清楚地区分了 trajectory hindsight 与 stateful foresight。随后看 Section 3 中 SKL-SD、SKL-RL 的训练目标和数据构造方式，重点判断状态表示如何归一化、相同状态如何聚合、多步后继证据如何截断。实验部分建议看 WebShop、ScienceWorld、ChessPuzzles 三类任务的差异：前两者偏交互环境，ChessPuzzles 更能检查是否只是记忆轨迹模板。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a structural weakness in experience-based LLM agents: most reflection methods summarize whole trajectories after the fact, which mixes reusable environment dynamics with path-specific accidents. The proposed alternative is to learn explicit predictive knowledge tied to states, so that the agent can reason about what the current state implies for future outcomes.","importance":"This is mainly an agent-learning and post-training paper, but it is relevant to pretraining researchers interested in experience data and continual learning. It argues that interaction traces should not be reduced to global reflections such as “avoid this strategy early.” A better training signal binds state, action context, successor evidence, and predictive statements. That framing is useful for designing agent SFT data, RL environments, memory systems, and synthetic experience corpora.","technical_approach":"Stateful Knowledge Learning maintains a state-indexed table of declarative knowledge z_s. For each visited state, the agent asks what that state predicts about future outcomes. During later updates, it aggregates multiple local future snippets around the same state, rather than summarizing a single full trajectory. The policy then conditions on both the current state and z_s when choosing actions. The paper also introduces two scalable variants. SKL-SD uses self-distillation to train the model to extract and update stateful knowledge from experience. SKL-RL uses reinforcement learning so the agent learns to aggregate state-level evidence and bootstrap predictive knowledge backward from successor states.","experiments":"A stochastic FrozenLake example illustrates why state-level predictive knowledge can generalize better than trajectory-level hindsight. In a slippery transition setting, a reflection such as “do not move right early” is brittle, while a statement such as “moving right is hazardous when a pit is adjacent” is tied to the actual state and can transfer. Larger experiments are reported on WebShop, ScienceWorld, and contamination-free ChessPuzzles. 角色拆分：challenger（proposer）生成带可验证 target 的问题；solver 用搜索工具解题并拿 reward。两者参数分离。\n2) 非参数技能记忆 B：存“人类可读”的技能条目，每条包含：u（技能描述）、c（触发条件）、a（avoidance cues/反例与易错点）、z（可复用 query 模板）、m（使用统计：检索/有用/有害计数），并且是有容量上限的 bounded memory。\n3) 非对称访问：solver 训练与推理可检索技能；challenger 看不到技能库，避免“出题方泄题/对抗失效”，维持不对称博弈。\n4) 四阶段闭环：\n- memory priming：用少量手写技能 + 早期 self-play 挖掘的去重技能初始化，给检索一个合理粒度与锚点。\n- asymmetric self-play：solver 在 rollout 时检索技能参与决策，使技能影响 on-policy 轨迹，而不是训练后再加 RAG。\n- frontier shaping：用某种机制把 challenger 推向 solver 能力边界附近（既不过易也不过难），让失败更“可学习”。\n- failure distillation：从“信息量高的失败”中提炼技能，去噪/去重后写回记忆，并用 m 统计做维护（抑制噪声与冗余）。\n5) 评估拆分 parametric vs non-parametric：训练后分别看 SESA-Off（关闭记忆，仅保留参数内化的收益）与 SESA-On（开启记忆，包含检索增益），并与纯 self-play baseline（SSP）对比，试图回答收益来自模型还是来自外部库。","experiments":"论文给出的关键信息：在 3,125 道 held-out 问题、覆盖 7 个 factual +
